# Changelog

## Latest

<!-- changelog-commit:20e62eb1a8deae984b884ff6051dc4f96a9978a2 -->
### Commit [`20e62eb`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/20e62eb1a8deae984b884ff6051dc4f96a9978a2)

```text
perf(schemas): load compact indexes and hydrate models on demand

Discover enabled operations without decoding every source or cloning full
models during startup and metadata changes. Load canonical operation models
and their reference closures on demand, bound cached working sets, and
release closed inspectors after their transition.

Preserve independent schema histories and factory ownership across bundled
updates. Migrate credential policies to targeted revision-bound records;
discovery descriptors cannot prepare or authorize requests.

Retain exact matching scores with normalized candidates and reusable edit
distance storage. Record paired browser heaps and CPU profiles, qualify all
13,859 service models against complete extraction, and cover storage and
inspection in Chromium, Firefox and WebKit. Native benchmark workers avoid
distorting tight-loop timings inside the test runner's VM.

Keep the single HTML delivery contract. Precomputed indexes add about 5.5%
gzip bytes while reducing fresh startup heap from 286 to 46 MiB.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:97efc3c75e10d5d0c25ebdbc3a5f7cfbb0bce622 -->
### Commit [`97efc3c`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/97efc3c75e10d5d0c25ebdbc3a5f7cfbb0bce622)

```text
perf(schemas): reuse search indexes and batch workflow models

Retain normalized indexes per schema and reuse completed matching workers. Send matched scores by default while preserving full developer reports and immediate termination of obsolete calculations.

Share navigation tooltip state and skip offscreen row contents while retaining native keyboard navigation. Group canonical workflow models into bounded schema batches with coalesced loads and atomic credential policy publication.

Add focused Gherkin coverage and record resource-capped measurements, including the retained search-worker heap tradeoff.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:060f88167fb2c692985be39aa9bebcd54893f72f -->
### Commit [`060f881`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/060f88167fb2c692985be39aa9bebcd54893f72f)

```text
docs(schemas): update performance roadmap and profiling findings

Record the completed develop integrations and prioritize cleaner phase measurements before further UI, matching, retention and model-projection work. Explain Playwright snapshot overhead, omitted matching-worker CPU sampling and unresolved startup and heap tradeoffs. Keep separate schema delivery deferred with its prerequisites.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:e1936ce62fddb72be902937676b9cd48b313e933 -->
### Commit [`e1936ce`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/e1936ce62fddb72be902937676b9cd48b313e933)

```text
perf(schemas): release hidden controls and compact matching work

Mount settings controls only in the visible section and release them after
closing. Preserve the Browser indicator, transitions and saved settings.
Retain exact compact search results without full candidate sorting; reuse
pending installations and completed menus while releasing closed workers.

Separate primary timing and heap measurements from diagnostic profiling.
Record startup, worker CPU, model-stage costs and rejected cache experiments.
Cover cancellation ownership, native navigation and Firefox error arguments.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:a5e3eb448bc1b7684f9b28fe508f1ed141920a9f -->
### Commit [`a5e3eb4`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/a5e3eb448bc1b7684f9b28fe508f1ed141920a9f)

```text
perf(workspace): accelerate exact search and workflow loading

Bound compact edit-distance work without changing returned scores or ties.
Delegate catalog help through one library trigger, retaining native row
navigation, disabled explanations, tooltip preferences and motion.

Decode embedded sources with native bytes and a checked compatibility path.
Record paired browser/native evidence and actual 96-operation restoration.
Schema delivery and canonical model independence remain unchanged.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:fbc238a2f14dff9e37112fef30e17cd8e2172ca3 -->
### Commit [`fbc238a`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/fbc238a2f14dff9e37112fef30e17cd8e2172ca3)

```text
fix(examples): describe placeholder schemas and HTTP echo requests plainly

Present the example.com catalog as illustrative OpenAPI shapes rather than
an implemented projects/tasks service. Describe httpbin forms as arbitrary
sample requests to its live echo endpoint, and use neutral sample values.

Retain operation IDs, paths, request keys and validation constraints for
existing workflows. Align existing browser assertions with the visible
labels and document the once-only example seeding boundary.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:24032b7de5703bf3ab625c5be676349757dc9086 -->
### Commit [`24032b7`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/24032b7de5703bf3ab625c5be676349757dc9086)

```text
feat(schemas): collapse document cards and group schema actions

Keep schema cards closed until opened, with name editing, enablement and
reordering available in the compact header. Move document actions into
the schema disclosure header while retaining the full viewer width.

Release the viewer when its card closes and explain the enable switch
with a tooltip for its current action.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:a3c14fdf5063554939127e796f90d3b14d690e9e -->
### Commit [`a3c14fd`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/a3c14fdf5063554939127e796f90d3b14d690e9e)

```text
Merge branch 'develop' into feature/schema-card-disclosure
```

<!-- changelog-commit:7a9567b65c89dd989050efde643d4cddfe88b5a9 -->
### Commit [`7a9567b`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/7a9567b65c89dd989050efde643d4cddfe88b5a9)

```text
feat(settings): stage Browser opening with a building indicator

Paint a compact Building... status and finish its initial slide before
constructing the Browser dashboard. Let the existing disclosure animate
to the built content height, and reuse the dashboard on later opens.
Cancel pending construction when closing or switching sections.

Cover the painted compact state, full expansion and reuse under normal
and reduced motion, plus cancellation before construction in Cucumber.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:d6976e7468773b40a42e61db6070ae0977c9de54 -->
### Commit [`d6976e7`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/d6976e7468773b40a42e61db6070ae0977c9de54)

```text
fix(settings): show readable building status on every Browser open

Use the expanded section's foreground for the Building... status instead
of inheriting the page foreground. Reset construction on every Browser
open so repeated opens follow the same compact status and expansion.

Verify both opens under normal and reduced motion in light and dark
themes, including the status foreground, and retain cancellation coverage.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:b7564cbaf181893d80af1004cbbc86920ba15bee -->
### Commit [`b7564cb`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/b7564cbaf181893d80af1004cbbc86920ba15bee)


<!-- changelog-commit:addb01f8a3e35e05717117dae63599ab43923358 -->
### Commit [`addb01f`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/addb01f8a3e35e05717117dae63599ab43923358)

```text
fix(settings): use three dot styles for Browser building status

```

<!-- changelog-commit:c7d29cb647827b83ba16a3c863650d1393a8becd -->
### Commit [`c7d29cb`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/c7d29cb647827b83ba16a3c863650d1393a8becd)

```text
feat(settings): animate a ring across five bold building dots

Replace the three-symbol Browser building status with five bold middle
dots and a ring that steps between them in both directions. Keep endpoint
positions visible before reversing. Reduced motion leaves the ring at
the center, and the accessible Building label stays unchanged.

Extend focused Cucumber checks to verify dot count, weight, ring marker
and the full animation path across repeated opens and both themes.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:ec212c4294269f3c672944f4710b8aea545bb2aa -->
### Commit [`ec212c4`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/ec212c4294269f3c672944f4710b8aea545bb2aa)

```text
fix(settings): keep three dots in the initial Browser pane

Replace the moving ring with three medium-sized bold middle dots. Mount
the cheap indicator before opening so it belongs to the initial slide.
Prepare the dashboard invisibly behind that compact pane, then reveal it
after mounting settles so nested content cannot jump its visible height.

Verify the compact and full height animations at intermediate positions,
alongside repeated opens, both themes, reduced motion and cancellation.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:a2a0f2f9ad7c5bed5a699ceed071fa3a4451a216 -->
### Commit [`a2a0f2f`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/a2a0f2f9ad7c5bed5a699ceed071fa3a4451a216)

```text
feat(settings): animate building dots in a scaling wave

Scale the three middle dots smoothly with staggered phases. Transform-only
animation preserves the compact panel dimensions; reduced motion keeps
the dots still. Retain the accessible Building status label.

Verify growth, peak, shrink and return to the initial scale, staggered
phases and reduced motion alongside the existing opening checks.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:8b0647e484f47058d571129597bc6f1799bf8e06 -->
### Commit [`8b0647e`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/8b0647e484f47058d571129597bc6f1799bf8e06)

```text
fix(settings): close building dots before expanding Browser content

Keep the constructed dashboard hidden while the compact dots pane slides
fully closed. Wait for that real height animation before revealing the
Browser view, so the histogram expands from zero instead of appearing
during the handoff. Cancel pending handoffs when switching sections.

Extend the focused browser test to inspect the closing animation and
verify dashboard invisibility before its separate opening animation.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:1ddbce539aea8bdb184e003817a90e2f9cd9d0d4 -->
### Commit [`1ddbce5`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/1ddbce539aea8bdb184e003817a90e2f9cd9d0d4)

```text
test: unify Gherkin execution and coverage in Vitest

Run every authored case through the maintained QuickPickle integration,
including former standalone browser scripts and Svelte lifecycle checks.
Preserve scenario identities, cancellation, cleanup and native diagnostics.

Publish Vitest's built-in report with coverage and attachments as one
verified directory. Bind complete runs to their cases, configuration,
installed toolchain and artifact hashes before release reuse or publication.

Retain the scoped 100% coverage gates and remove two unreachable source
alternatives without changing application behavior.

```

## 0.0.51

<!-- changelog-commit:be052a07c443177cb2bfaee5329abef33c7ba463 -->
### Commit [`be052a0`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/be052a07c443177cb2bfaee5329abef33c7ba463)

```text
fix(workflows): retain focus across repeated undo and redo

Do not replay navigation autofocus when a workflow action stops being
busy. Restore canvas focus if temporary inertness cleared it during a
history operation, without overriding an intentional move to another
control. Preserve native text undo in Search.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:0b6cca8705bbd29dab37a0cb92835b7a9b43c717 -->
### Commit [`0b6cca8`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/0b6cca8705bbd29dab37a0cb92835b7a9b43c717)

```text
feat(workflows): add typed JSON and CSV file upload nodes

Parse bounded uploads in disposable workers and persist their data with the
workflow. Infer native JSON output types and expose CSV rows as arrays while
preserving cell text, with editable delimiter, header and quote options.

Reject unsupported or malformed files without replacing existing data, remove
incompatible mappings after replacement, and include file values in execution.
Exclude file-picker events from unfinished text-edit tracking so uploads save.

Validate with focused Cucumber source and browser scenarios, complete parser
coverage, dependency documentation checks, type checking and production build.

```

```text
feat(workflows): resize comment nodes from side and corner handles

Replace the native textarea grip with eight themed resize controls on selected
comments. Persist bounded dimensions with the node and retain them across text
edits, exports, reloads and workflow history. Save each completed resize as a
single edit rather than recording intermediate pointer movements.

Validate dimensions through Cucumber source coverage and check resize/reload
and existing comment editing in two focused Chromium scenarios. Type checking
and the production build pass.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:ad949965f453ee92cec056914df3badc8b838324 -->
### Commit [`ad94996`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/ad949965f453ee92cec056914df3badc8b838324)

```text
feat(workflows): bound file schema previews and load more on demand

Limit the file schema scroll area to five visible rows. Render an initial
1,000-line tree budget shared across expanded branches and expose Load more
for successive batches. Reset the budget on document replacement and retain
existing unpaginated behavior for other schema views.

Type checking, production build and two focused Chromium Cucumber scenarios
pass, covering bounded rendering, pagination, replacement and upload persistence.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:4537d695a764f2ff9a810eea7b383a27881c3523 -->

### Commit [`26277a7`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/26277a73c19fecaa890ae51d5c74859ef33b5ef7)

```text
fix(workflows): triple the file preview viewport height

```

<!-- changelog-commit:80467d938367cf9341b19db427dde173f2b99a31 -->
### Commit [`80467d9`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/80467d938367cf9341b19db427dde173f2b99a31)

```text
feat(workflows): project uploaded data into connected output structures

Infer bounded data shapes from uploaded JSON and CSV and reuse operation output
panels and placement. Expose nested object fields and array projections as usable
outputs. Preserve stable field identities, panel positions and compatible mappings
when replacing files, removing obsolete panels and incompatible connections.

Treat file-owned output fields as static sources during workflow execution,
including explicit starting operations. Keep array projections typed as arrays.

Ten focused Cucumber source scenarios and two Chromium scenarios pass, covering
nested execution, CSV column requests, replacement reconciliation and persistence.
Type checking and production build pass.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:51afee61f0718db5c45ace90b0b959e997a8e58f -->
### Commit [`51afee6`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/51afee61f0718db5c45ace90b0b959e997a8e58f)

```text
feat(workflows): show array icons and item ranges

Use Data Array for array panels and display cardinality badges with zero as
the default minimum and an asterisk for unknown maximum. Preserve OpenAPI
minItems/maxItems and derive uploaded array maxima from their data, including
array projections such as CSV columns. Save and validate this metadata with
workflow fields and panels.

Eighteen focused source scenarios, four Chromium scenarios, type checking
and production build pass.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:4756f2c2fa2edefad0d938f9a4175c3efb00e4f9 -->
### Commit [`4756f2c`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/4756f2c2fa2edefad0d938f9a4175c3efb00e4f9)

```text
fix(workflows): show array bounds in Items labels and restore type badges

```

<!-- changelog-commit:c8cba91d24dc02af117fe15a6b08d5b3b3b449cd -->
### Commit [`c8cba91`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/c8cba91d24dc02af117fe15a6b08d5b3b3b449cd)

```text
feat(workflows): infer numeric CSV columns from all data rows

Convert a column to numbers only when every cell is a finite, safely represented
decimal value. Keep the entire column as text when any cell is nonnumeric or
empty. Infer Integer or Number item attributes and preserve array-valued output
connections, including persisted item type metadata.

Update request and persistence checks for actual numeric values. Twelve focused
source scenarios pass with complete parser coverage; two Chromium scenarios,
type checking and production build pass.

```

### Commit [`d6df114`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/d6df1144cb254bf8cb80c26f069526024ce0e6e2)


<!-- changelog-commit:6f53822cb4c76a9c09353e1845786c6829eeba9a -->
### Commit [`6f53822`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/6f53822cb4c76a9c09353e1845786c6829eeba9a)

```text
feat(workflows): execute file Item connections once per aligned row

Match file Item connections by their displayed scalar type and retain whole
column mappings for array inputs. Execute the selected path sequentially per
row, including downstream operations and nested calls, without mixing columns
or repeating unrelated starting paths. Apply the operation guard per row.

Preserve the current row on retry, skip filtered or empty rows and reject unequal
row counts from combined sources. Cache immutable file projections across row
frames instead of repeatedly cloning entire columns for scalar requests.

Validate with 21 focused file-source scenarios, selected existing runner checks,
and three Chromium upload/execution scenarios. Type checking and production
build pass; full regression remains with CI.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:3c2cf3f908d93c4c37153c5fe57b25fa60189d32 -->
### Commit [`3c2cf3f`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/3c2cf3f908d93c4c37153c5fe57b25fa60189d32)

```text
fix(workflows): recover transient file worker startup failures

Prepare Papa Parse during development dependency optimization and retry
read-only parsing once with a fresh worker after a worker failure. Keep
parse errors and cancellation immediate, and bound persistent failures.

Exercise actual Vite development workers in Cucumber, including cold
loading, failed starts, later recovery, JSON/CSV parsing, option changes,
and the visible file upload node. Production-only checks missed this path.

```

### Commit [`c8502e4`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/c8502e4499d2b4b6d53f45e413d41ba5af3df2b5)

```text
feat(workflows): add connectable vault variable nodes

Add a Variable Add toolbar action and a vault-entry picker with Text output\nconnections. Persist only entry IDs and strip local selections from portable\nworkflows, including nested snapshots.\n\nResolve variable values in the vault worker immediately before validated\nrequest dispatch. Keep previews sanitized, including encoded path values,\nand upgrade schema approvals to support operations without authentication.\n\nCover reference validation, connection eligibility, nested and additional\nfields, locked sessions, export cleanup and native worker execution with\nfocused Cucumber scenarios.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:7693d78ffd902a45e9ef3aed3a595cba028606b7 -->
### Commit [`7693d78`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/7693d78ffd902a45e9ef3aed3a595cba028606b7)

```text
fix(workflows): wrap toolbar beside the open navigation menu

Reserve the desktop navigation width when sizing and packing workflow
controls. Recompute on menu toggles, including reduced-motion layouts,
while preserving the overlaid menu and unchanged canvas geometry.

Extend focused browser checks to cover visible toolbar actions and compact
rows beside navigation at desktop widths, plus existing Settings wrapping.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:7391792ded8aa7d782e01e96a74612e0c5f1bc76 -->
### Commit [`7391792`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/7391792ded8aa7d782e01e96a74612e0c5f1bc76)

```text
fix(settings): block workspace editing when the drawer leaves little room

Make the workflow inactive while Settings occupies a viewport below
1220px, leaving desktop editing available when at least 320px remains.
Keep the guard through the closing animation and update it on resize,
moving canvas focus into Settings when the available space disappears.

Check narrow and desktop interaction, shortcut isolation, resizing and
closing with focused browser scenarios.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:8f15038e6de7730361b471bf19b21e788a87f661 -->
### Commit [`8f15038`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/8f15038e6de7730361b471bf19b21e788a87f661)

```text
fix(schemas): expand operation methods in the Settings viewer

Open HTTP method branches alongside their paths by default so operation
summaries and identifiers are visible immediately. Keep deeper request
and response structures collapsed and restore these defaults on reopening.

Verify the method overview and reopen behavior with a focused browser case.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:bf89a4b2eb6085b32d6946e0e1d0b93f65398258 -->
### Commit [`bf89a4b`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/bf89a4b2eb6085b32d6946e0e1d0b93f65398258)

```text
feat(workflows): place utility nodes near the visible viewport center

Find free space nearest the current visible workspace center for vault
variables, comments, switches and casts using their rendered dimensions.
Prefer fully visible placements, then search beyond the viewport when
needed, respecting grid snapping and preserving existing node positions.

Pan to center the inserted utility beside open panes and below controls.
Keep operation trees on their existing ordered placement path.

Verify crowded and translated layouts against an exhaustive search and
exercise all four utility types through the browser toolbar.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:2a76993f6212fd985d6bf79a498f2a0656cfcbb8 -->
### Commit [`2a76993`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/2a76993f6212fd985d6bf79a498f2a0656cfcbb8)

```text
feat(vault): expose public key names without unlocking values

Persist a bounded public index of entry names, IDs and kinds alongside
atomic encrypted vault updates. Keep values and grants encrypted and
resolve workflow references only through authenticated worker records.

Allow locked key browsing in Settings and variable pickers. Require an
unlocked session for copying, mutations and workflow value use. Rebuild
the index during import or the next unlock of a legacy vault, without
rewriting encrypted snapshots just to publish names.

Coalesce concurrent status reads and refresh public metadata without
restarting secret workers on lock notifications. Update documentation and
focused Cucumber checks for visibility, value guards and migration.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:c136e054eb64c69ca06dd54785a7cebca109b6bf -->

```text
Keep public vault keys selectable through an inline dropdown, including
while locked. Show the String type beside the output connection, and
preserve the unlock fallback for legacy vault metadata.

```

## 0.0.49

<!-- changelog-commit:96275fe830d174c5e91d6a882cddfda1733f0944 -->
### Commit [`96275fe`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/96275fe830d174c5e91d6a882cddfda1733f0944)

```text
test(browser): reduce setup and resource measurement waits

Batch palette accessibility checks and use bounded native keyboard routes, including WebKit's non-wrapping behavior. Finish only the ready loader's presentation during shared setup, preserving real initialization and cleanup. Supply immediate page-memory responses only to storage inspection cases, retaining native memory coverage, fresh contexts and storage assertions.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:f734472ea519d5488619da70a4d93049f0a0822b -->
### Commit [`f734472`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/f734472ea519d5488619da70a4d93049f0a0822b)

```text
test(browser): narrow fixtures and repeated palette checks

Keep vault management coverage in its explicit creation scenario instead of repeating key rotation during unrelated setup. Separate real Git repository fixtures from editor activation and remove their unconditional reload.

Check full palette structure in both theme modes while using targeted selection and persistence assertions for repeated interactions. Poll completed autosaves, retaining real pauses for negative timing assertions. Document these boundaries without changing application code, CI or cryptographic costs.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:711c124a19f82a34cf129094a00ec0a001189f44 -->
### Commit [`711c124`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/711c124a19f82a34cf129094a00ec0a001189f44)

```text
test(browser): start isolated scenarios on fixture pages

Route fixture-only vault, error-storage, recorder and coverage scenarios directly to their own entry pages instead of initializing and discarding the application. Remove redundant first-step navigation while preserving explicit reloads and additional-tab setup.

Keep application startup as the default for UI and startup scenarios, along with fresh contexts, request guards, diagnostics, traces and production cryptographic parameters. Document the fixture tag boundaries.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:5e70122f4655d78748184379033a393e5c1f8b03 -->
### Commit [`5e70122`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/5e70122f4655d78748184379033a393e5c1f8b03)

```text
test(browser): load graph fixtures through native import

Generate schema graphs for shared connection and presentation setup and load them through the real import/autosave path. Reuse that path for drag fixtures instead of restarting the application.

Retain explicit catalog creation and measured insertion scenarios, real pointer gestures, persistence reload assertions, and repository setup for identity/history fixtures. Document the fresh document identity and estimated fixture layout boundaries.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:5f12f5d8df3b11b0bcc62103b77c904d08931290 -->
### Commit [`5f12f5d`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/5f12f5d8df3b11b0bcc62103b77c904d08931290)

```text
test(browser): prepare fixture bundles once per invocation

Build the five standalone fixtures serially in the browser test coordinator and share one invocation-specific artifact with all Cucumber workers. Keep the existing build settings, native browser behavior and report formats.

Route the Docker runner through the public Cucumber API entry point. Skip preparation for dry runs and remove temporary bundles after success, scenario failure or build failure. Document direct execution and include worker/artifact metadata for verification.

```

## 0.0.48

<!-- changelog-commit:90d9b39dcc0a46d623f8dc2b32008320ad923d01 -->
### Commit [`90d9b39`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/90d9b39dcc0a46d623f8dc2b32008320ad923d01)

```text
fix(startup): recover from failed development module loads

Catch application import failures before the settings, workers and error
reporter load. Retry recognized development module-fetch failures once per
page, with a session guard to prevent reload loops; otherwise expose the
original error and a manual Reload action. Preserve mounted applications.

Keep startup orchestration behind an explicit initializer and let development
error overlays appear above the loader. Add focused Cucumber coverage for
storage guards and real Vite module failures. Carry the existing orchestration
coverage scope forward and update the scenario count using prior CI evidence
plus the new passing focused cases; full regression remains with CI.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:b86a382e44b03e1736c09bfe3a2c6a47a45fb20d -->
### Commit [`b86a382`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/b86a382e44b03e1736c09bfe3a2c6a47a45fb20d)

```text
Merge branch 'develop' into bugfix/startup-module-recovery
```

<!-- changelog-commit:4321c396526a390e46f725f204dbe8c2766e37d9 -->
### Commit [`4321c39`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/4321c396526a390e46f725f204dbe8c2766e37d9)

```text
feat(workflows): unify operation discovery and execution

Move prompt matching into navigation with cancellable worker evaluation
and retained catalog groups. Menu selections add nodes, and HTTP
execution runs through Run workflow.

Share typed forms, field help, request previews, credential controls and
actual response handling. Preserve mapped inputs and allow cancelling a
pending request without ending the run.

Replace obsolete wizard checks with Cucumber coverage for live discovery
and adapt execution, navigation and documentation to the unified flow.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:fbf84eba387c331ae92117fbffd091091cc91b30 -->
### Commit [`fbf84eb`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/fbf84eba387c331ae92117fbffd091091cc91b30)

```text
feat(workflows): add field information and value presence controls

Expose schema details for every run field and inline controls to omit
optional values or send an allowed null without discarding input drafts.

Preserve explicit null and omission through OpenAPI normalization, request
validation, workflow mappings and credential execution. Connected inputs
remain immutable, and nullable enum and required constraints still apply.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:b326586bfad53878d0f5e9c0d4ad6668da200191 -->
### Commit [`b326586`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/b326586bfad53878d0f5e9c0d4ad6668da200191)

```text
fix(navigation): hide empty categories in prompt matches

Prune catalog branches without matching operations and restore all categories when the prompt is cleared. Cover nested pruning, empty results and restoration in the live prompt scenarios.

```

```text
fix(secrets): avoid layout shifts when saving idle preferences

Save inactivity preferences independently of vault operations so toggles and slider commits do not insert progress text or cancellation controls. Retain guarded error handling and prevent overlapping preference writes without clearing credential inputs.

```

```text
fix(sliders): derive hover scaling from measured dimensions

Centralize hover scaling in FieldInteger and derive each scale factor from its live untransformed dimensions. Sliders expand by eight pixels horizontally and two vertically at every responsive width. Remove per-section percentage overrides and preserve reduced-motion behavior.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:da5369889c0efe20f1c8010b98803f3fa4a74c5b -->
### Commit [`da53698`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/da5369889c0efe20f1c8010b98803f3fa4a74c5b)

```text
feat(secrets): allow vault passwords from three characters

Apply the three-character minimum to vault creation and password enrollment in both the form and worker. Update the focused recovery-confirmation scenario to accept three characters and reject shorter passwords.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:1240db52842245ca06f9e65583310c74d674b5b1 -->
### Commit [`1240db5`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/1240db52842245ca06f9e65583310c74d674b5b1)

```text
feat(secrets): simplify recovery saving and lengthen new keys

Remove the recovery re-entry requirement and save through a right-aligned download action. Move guidance below the key and remove the pane heading and cancellation button. Generate 128-byte recovery keys while accepting legacy 32-byte keys. Update focused crypto and browser scenarios for direct saving and downloads.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:b21e1b0f46659d66fd1fc470e14ef0f2bdc1fe09 -->
### Commit [`b21e1b0`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/b21e1b0f46659d66fd1fc470e14ef0f2bdc1fe09)

```text
feat(secrets): display recovery key with a copy action

Render the recovery key in a wrapping pre block with a Content Copy action in its upper-right corner. Clear its DOM text when the recovery pane closes and adapt focused browser coverage to verify copying and export/import with the new presentation.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:4832a41ab11ec4df27b46126ea13f98a6b93129e -->
### Commit [`4832a41`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/4832a41ab11ec4df27b46126ea13f98a6b93129e)


### Commit [`6110750`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/6110750bbc4108de1ac8a533b951b1dee4e9d423)

```text
style(secrets): tighten recovery key spacing

```

<!-- changelog-commit:375cba7575d9e83974aaf1440e4257734b4f9e5c -->
### Commit [`375cba7`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/375cba7575d9e83974aaf1440e4257734b4f9e5c)

```text
style(secrets): remove unlocked status and manual lock button

Hide the status pane while the vault is unlocked and remove its manual lock action. Retain locked and absent-vault guidance and cancellation for pending locked operations. Adapt browser checks to management readiness and page-exit locking.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:023cc990ec04259e53565bbe7fcf70da59fd3b1f -->
### Commit [`023cc99`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/023cc990ec04259e53565bbe7fcf70da59fd3b1f)

```text
feat(secrets): store new values without choosing a credential type

Remove the type selector and type badges. New generic secrets use the approved OpenAPI security scheme for request formatting; legacy typed entries retain their compatibility restrictions. Preserve exact destination grants and cover generic persistence, both header formats and refusal without approval.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:7167ad76a2119a07554dd6a78f384da92af36072 -->
### Commit [`7167ad7`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/7167ad76a2119a07554dd6a78f384da92af36072)

```text
fix(secrets): separate recovery keys from password saving

Give vault password forms an explicit non-secret username instead of leaving password managers to infer one. Keep recovery saving outside form submission, distinguish recovery authentication inputs and recreate them when switching methods. Verify the username metadata, recovery isolation and vault export/import in a focused browser scenario.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:623849653acd28474e40698e4d3ac8edde23a96d -->
### Commit [`6238496`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/623849653acd28474e40698e4d3ac8edde23a96d)

```text
style(secrets): remove locked vault status pane

Show the introductory status pane only before a vault exists. Update browser assertions to recognize the unlock form instead of the removed locked status.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:f0e2643d33a5047e628db1d5e37aa5a792a17c6e -->
### Commit [`f0e2643`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/f0e2643d33a5047e628db1d5e37aa5a792a17c6e)

```text
style(secrets): place vault replacement guidance beside its action

Remove the exports pane heading and label existing-vault imports as Replace vault. Align the explanation to the right of that button while retaining export access and no-vault import wording.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:407b149ccc7d82060698625aadf8299396e0bc00 -->
### Commit [`407b149`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/407b149ccc7d82060698625aadf8299396e0bc00)

```text
feat(secrets): export nonempty vaults before replacement

Authenticate and download the current encrypted snapshot before replacing a vault containing records. Skip downloads for empty vaults and support current-vault authentication while locked. Bind replacement to the exported snapshot so intervening changes abort instead of losing unexported data. Cover the worker guards and actual replacement download with focused Cucumber scenarios.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:bc72eba7f2113e443d702e27a424c2a51b62b08e -->
### Commit [`bc72eba`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/bc72eba7f2113e443d702e27a424c2a51b62b08e)

```text
style(secrets): remove vault unlock heading

```

<!-- changelog-commit:5ef2f53bdab367f0f415b46ef2aab30abc7f1874 -->
### Commit [`5ef2f53`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/5ef2f53bdab367f0f415b46ef2aab30abc7f1874)

```text
style(secrets): right align vault unlock button

```

<!-- changelog-commit:a4759d48b69c337bddc796df95fc0aefbf6e1a0e -->
### Commit [`a4759d4`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/a4759d48b69c337bddc796df95fc0aefbf6e1a0e)

```text
fix(secrets)!: accept only 128-byte recovery keys

Remove the legacy 32-byte recovery-key path. Require exactly 128 decoded bytes and cover rejection of former keys and adjacent sizes alongside current unlock, import and enrollment behavior.

BREAKING CHANGE: Recovery keys issued in the former 32-byte format no longer unlock vaults or exports.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:8d078b74b57380484597e714fb0d0c19a93ce788 -->
### Commit [`8d078b7`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/8d078b74b57380484597e714fb0d0c19a93ce788)

```text
feat(secrets): infer unlock methods from the provided input

Replace the unlock-method dropdown with one password-or-recovery field and separate passkey access. Try 172-character inputs against registered recovery methods first, then saved passwords, preserving long passwords and multiple methods. Retain authenticated envelope and manifest checks and cover automatic recovery unlocking through the browser.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:d5d1b6d304bd15fca32cdf44d0d2db51e6a552c0 -->
### Commit [`d5d1b6d`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/d5d1b6d304bd15fca32cdf44d0d2db51e6a552c0)

```text
style(secrets): join unlock action to the authentication field

Place the unlock submit button against the right edge of the password-or-recovery input with shared height, joined corners and an enclosing focus outline. Preserve passkey submission and verify adjacency alongside successful browser unlocking.

```

```text
style(secrets): slide rotation controls below a dashed divider

Animate rotation fields open and closed over 240ms with ease-in-out and respect reduced motion. Separate the expanding content from its header with eight-pixel dashes and six-pixel gaps. Verify opening and cancellation preserve saved vault data.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:fca601b4af210dccb01ffdec9d8f7a6fe06ab046 -->
### Commit [`fca601b`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/fca601b4af210dccb01ffdec9d8f7a6fe06ab046)

```text
feat(secrets): regenerate recovery keys automatically during rotation

Verify retained passwords and passkeys without requesting old recovery keys or displaying single-option selectors. Replace recovery envelopes with one automatically generated 128-byte key and present it for copying or download after rotation. Share the fixed key size across generation, validation and unlock inference. Cover retained access, renewed recovery, atomic interruption and the browser rotation/export/import flow.

```

```text
fix(secrets): download new recovery keys after rotation

Trigger the recovery-key download immediately after successful rotation and keep its presentation available for copying or another download. Reuse the same download helper as recovery saving and verify the automatic file contains exactly the generated key. Measure joined-control alignment in one frame to avoid scroll timing differences.

```

```text
Run unlocking without inserting a temporary status message. Preserve existing notices during silent operations instead of removing their layout space while authentication is pending. Keep busy-state protection and error reporting.

```

```text
feat(secrets): give passkey enrollment its own authentication pane

Make Add passkey open a sliding pane with local authentication and a joined verification action, matching rotation controls. Support cancellation and clear local secrets on closure or locking. Show enrollment failures beside the action without temporary loading messages. Update browser scenarios and verify supported and unsupported native PRF authenticator flows.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:43c59e9a3c9cfd6e31b481842154c599faf22bcb -->

```text
fix(secrets): explain unavailable passkey encryption accurately

Distinguish unavailable browser or provider PRF support during enrollment from vault failures. State that the existing vault is unchanged and slide the inline error open and closed with reduced-motion support. Verify the exact message against a native authenticator without PRF.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:4d6dec40adac3563af1076d3973da8664799ead9 -->
### Commit [`4d6dec4`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/4d6dec40adac3563af1076d3973da8664799ead9)

```text
fix(secrets): limit vaults to one generated recovery key

Remove the recovery add-or-replace controls and preparation RPC. Recovery keys are generated only during creation or rotation; public method enrollment rejects them. Reject snapshots with multiple recovery envelopes and cover the invariant alongside rotation, unlocking and the browser settings flow.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:eee4258acaea1c37f8f3af4e31c664b0493b57ab -->
### Commit [`eee4258`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/eee4258acaea1c37f8f3af4e31c664b0493b57ab)

```text
feat(secrets): replace passwords in a dedicated expandable pane

Match password replacement to the other vault actions with a sliding pane, cancellation, local authentication and new-password confirmation. Join the submit icon to the confirmation field and show validation errors locally without loading text. Allow current-password or recovery authentication so forgotten passwords remain replaceable, and replace the existing method instead of adding another by default. Verify mismatch safety, old-password rejection and unchanged recovery access in a focused browser scenario.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:702439cee795436181baa4ec8e0f43447538ef62 -->

```text
fix(secrets): export encrypted vaults without password verification

Allow direct ciphertext downloads without unlocking, decrypting or changing storage. Keep export available while locked and avoid temporary progress messages. Preserve authentication for replacement and import, and verify locked exports remain encrypted and restore through a valid recovery key.

```

## 0.0.47

<!-- changelog-commit:819912ae378090707b77d540e6b0494736481017 -->
### Commit [`819912a`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/819912ae378090707b77d540e6b0494736481017)

```text
fix(ci): serialize integrations and refresh queued candidates

Hold one retained queue across integration validation, merging and
publication so concurrent candidates cannot invalidate each other's base.
Resolve live target revisions before testing, refresh topic branches with
an expected-head update, and let their new event validate the new source.

Skip obsolete queued events, retain source-preserving synchronization and
bind reusable validation to the executing workflow definitions. Preserve
explicit merged-release publication retries without requiring deleted
source branches or reopening changelog work.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:58e7706de0867a1a9daa6a661c8a00fb5ce3e9ab -->
### Commit [`58e7706`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/58e7706de0867a1a9daa6a661c8a00fb5ce3e9ab)

```text
fix(ci): align changelog checks and refresh scenario badges

Require prepared integration inputs in the older changelog workflow test,
including its live target and tested revision. Preserve its route and
heavy-test isolation checks.

Render the 1,774-scenario badge from unchanged application reports in run
38639551443, automation run 38760044368 and the actual focused retest of
its one corrected assertion. Preserve the original reports; this combined
evidence is not a new full-run validation proof. CI still validates the
complete corrected revision.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:9cffa93ec0b07a7807f96fa706a9989f21c81953 -->
### Commit [`9cffa93`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/9cffa93ec0b07a7807f96fa706a9989f21c81953)

```text
feat(secrets): add an encrypted browser credential vault

Keep saved API credentials outside workflow and schema history. Encrypt the
local vault with AES-GCM, protect password unlock with pinned Argon2id, and
provide independent recovery and PRF-capable passkey methods.

Execute credentialed requests in a dedicated worker under explicit operation
and destination approvals. Reject stale schema grants, redirects and header
collisions; bound responses and diagnostics and sanitize known secret echoes.

Add Secrets settings, opaque local workflow bindings, atomic encrypted
backup/restore and key rotation. Lock on hidden-page and lifecycle events,
cancel late work and enforce optional idle deadlines across worker requests.

Cover crypto, policy, persistence, lifecycle and native browser behavior through
Cucumber. Document the origin-local trust boundary and unverified physical
mobile and passkey-provider compatibility.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:cbbd09bbf0c7384102d18fc22e277f7955babc17 -->
### Commit [`cbbd09b`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/cbbd09bbf0c7384102d18fc22e277f7955babc17)

```text
fix(ci): refresh vault scenario badges from completed CI

Regenerate both badge formats from the four Cucumber reports produced by run 38751508134 for 7c493a8. All 1,972 reported scenarios passed; no test results are synthesized or suites rerun locally.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:79f9a6d6ddb0508017a4ba68ac3112e2f0c4cfe2 -->
### Commit [`79f9a6d`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/79f9a6d6ddb0508017a4ba68ac3112e2f0c4cfe2)

```text
chore: synchronize browser vault with develop

Bring in the validated integration queue before publishing the vault update. Preserve both changelog histories and regenerate the combined 2,087-scenario badge from actual vault application and queue automation CI reports. Application inputs match the vault run, and automation tests and workflows match develop.

```

```text
fix(workflows): keep connection ports above selected edges

Selecting a node raised its connected edge above the opposite output port.
The edge's invisible hit area intercepted the next drag and panned the canvas.
Keep edges below node controls and use the shared connection gesture check
so the routing scenario verifies the source hit and live connection preview.

Refresh the scenario badge for the combined workflow and settings changes.

```

## 0.0.45

<!-- changelog-commit:58bebe05eea9512142065372084bd7d819cc4c06 -->
### Commit [`58bebe0`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/58bebe05eea9512142065372084bd7d819cc4c06)

```text
fix(ci): reuse validation proofs with bounded network retries

Rely on the original successful run and complete manifest instead of
listing its jobs again. Preserve input, provenance and artifact checks
while allowing transient reads and downloads two retries with 30-second
attempts inside the existing 120-second lookup budget.

Clear partial downloads before retrying and report safe failure categories.
Cover subprocess timeouts, retry exhaustion, cleanup and the workflow
gates that ensure only complete fresh validation can publish proof.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:364d8bd6d1630f25833ea2471df77506aa62703c -->
### Commit [`364d8bd`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/364d8bd6d1630f25833ea2471df77506aa62703c)

```text
chore: merge develop after release changelog synchronization

```

## 0.0.44

<!-- changelog-commit:6ef72c23fa2daf336ed692ee0748c8dccbdb2f78 -->
### Commit [`6ef72c2`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/6ef72c23fa2daf336ed692ee0748c8dccbdb2f78)

```text
ci(release): version changelogs by PR and reuse complete validation

Collect unreleased commit messages under Latest and preserve the source
snapshot when creating a release. Open a separate Markdown-only PR into
the release branch to assign its version before publication. Resume the
release only for that exact canonical changelog transition, including an
empty Latest section, and retain compatibility with existing Staging data.

Produce complete test, coverage and build evidence during integration.
Reuse it across later merges only when every tracked input except the root
changelog matches and the successful original run and artifact hashes
verify. Bound lookup time, fall back to fresh validation when evidence is
unavailable, and retain the original report provenance.

Keep release gates, retry protection, regression scenarios, documentation
and the scenario badge aligned with the new lifecycle.

```

```text
Document the recovery scope and the missing 0.0.21 tag, whose changes
first appear in the confirmed 0.0.22 tag.
```

<!-- changelog-commit:d068f6bf3a3a8a6cdb5342cb124bd2552e5ef6fb -->
### Commit [`d068f6b`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/d068f6bf3a3a8a6cdb5342cb124bd2552e5ef6fb)

```text
feat(ci): support explicit documentation merge targets

Allow Markdown-only PRs into active releases as well as main and develop,
with independent full-diff validation and the existing main freshness guard.
Serialize release documentation against production merges and keep
Markdown-only release advances from resuming publication.

Provide explicit recovery of a merged documentation PR's original source
head so squash merges and branch deletion do not broaden later targets.
Document reuse, rollout and retry behavior with Cucumber regression coverage.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:6108eb9a9c03c5a625c0b99e516b4cd4b4cbe904 -->

```text
feat(errors): retain bounded browser error history

Capture application, runtime, rejection and worker diagnostics without changing their existing console and UI reporting. Keep 1,000 unique errors in IndexedDB with occurrence counts and atomic FIFO eviction across tabs.

Record through a dedicated worker with bounded pending work, guarded failures and explicit retry. Expose paginated history, stacks and causes in Browser settings. Cover capture, persistence, concurrency and recovery with Cucumber scenarios.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:df6803cb23e9799f255859c8b7a595775ee444d4 -->

```text
test: migrate standalone source tests to Cucumber

Report all 78 existing Node test cases as individual source scenarios while
preserving their assertions and restoring shared state after each case.
Remove the separate test:unit path so source execution, coverage, reports
and scenario badges use the same Cucumber results.

Verify explicit source registration and real runner failure propagation,
including cleanup-hook failures, through generated HTML and badge outputs.
Update contributor guidance while retaining the separate browser smoke
migration gap.

```

## 0.0.42

<!-- changelog-commit:e2551bfd987b9ce39bc7853e90b2b05994494a71 -->
### Commit [`e2551bf`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/e2551bfd987b9ce39bc7853e90b2b05994494a71)

```text
feat(ci): preserve commit messages in staged release changelogs

Capture complete commit messages in CHANGELOG.md before feature, bugfix
and documentation branches are squashed into develop. Keep unreleased
entries under Staging and exclude generated changelog commits on retries.

Fold Staging into the chosen version before release validation. Further
develop integrations update the existing release version while preserving
release-only fixes. Verify pinned revisions and use expected-head pushes
to avoid publishing over concurrent changes.

Keep an explicit Staging boundary so concurrent branch merges cannot
silently classify unreleased entries under a published version.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:55ae4267aca4cc018b01d0deae3287144901aa56 -->
### Commit [`55ae426`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/55ae4267aca4cc018b01d0deae3287144901aa56)

```text
feat(workflows): add manual connection editing and controls

Let users adjust orthogonal connection segments with pointer or keyboard
controls while respecting node clearance. Preserve authored routes through
save, import, undo and redo, with automatic routing when a route is blocked.

Expose dash length, speed and node symbols in Workflow settings, separate
node clearance from curved lines, and promote the chosen browser defaults.
Retain existing saved preferences and workflow options.

Keep connection editing consistent with single-input occupancy, declared
type compatibility, logical tab order and visible focus controls. Include
the related Cucumber coverage, documentation and scenario badges.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:054c75b89152dd1d47bd4872e1acdf5c20935c3c -->

```text

Add directory guides for source, components, domain services, tests, automation
and architecture records, with a root project map and relevant ADR links.

verbatim and the release contract permits only index.html and _headers.
Preserve existing automation procedures and font provenance.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:fb4c127fc87e17717692dd030444c5dd7f1a14f8 -->
### Commit [`fb4c127`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/fb4c127fc87e17717692dd030444c5dd7f1a14f8)

```text
feat(workflows): route edges around nodes with synchronized dashes

Port the orthogonal routing module into a bounded dedicated worker while
preserving measured handle attachment, connection snapping and curved paths.
Share dash phases in world coordinates and let Workflow settings control
node clearance without persisting generated routes.

Retain body-avoiding fallbacks in crowded layouts, reject obsolete worker
replies, and preserve previews when connections are released. Include the
reference regressions and browser checks with the scoped coverage contract.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:82e42906cbf188277675f4dd1d6603af6b797c62 -->

## 0.0.41

<!-- changelog-commit:b9aee00069a6c5740bc2ac1a272fe9826a2a519d -->
### Commit [`b9aee00`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/b9aee00069a6c5740bc2ac1a272fe9826a2a519d)

```text
feat(ci): add markdown-only documentation integration

Give docs/* pull requests a trusted Markdown-only path to develop or main,
without tests, builds, releases, deployments, or branch synchronization.
Inspect complete Git diffs, including both rename paths and file modes,
before granting merge credentials.

Preserve guarded merge behavior, protect active production releases, and
exclude documentation branches from dependency checks and badge commits.
Include the scoped Actions event policy and normal-CI rollout guidance.

```

## 0.0.39

<!-- changelog-commit:5081f7618147615f1f80d8d517a7dee19d09f146 -->
### Commit [`5081f76`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/5081f7618147615f1f80d8d517a7dee19d09f146)

```text
feat(ui): refine operation execution and theme selection

Connect the Operation action to the shared HTTP executor and show real
responses, errors and retry controls. Both catalogs send requests to their
declared service, while fixtures remain explicitly isolated in tests.

Align catalog navigation, phase states and reports around the selected
operation. Propose the top five matches plus every result above 50%, expose
schema server URLs in metadata, and improve JSON layout and scope feedback.

Group themes in a collapsible Settings section with revised palettes and an
animated selection award. Keep header content aligned while its background
spans the page, and refine shared spacing, colors and pane transitions.

Preserve operation IDs, saved workflows and palette preference keys. These
interactions share UI state, semantic roles and browser assertions, so their
source, documentation and tests are recorded together.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:4890c87759d2f59c53e074d6b0e4667c18429093 -->
### Commit [`4890c87`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/4890c87759d2f59c53e074d6b0e4667c18429093)

```text
feat(workflow): add browser-local history and refine the editor

Persist each workflow in its own Git repository on a shared IndexedDB
filesystem. Autosave completed edits, preserve abandoned redo history,
and expose useful change messages and non-destructive restoration.

Move theme and workflow settings to IndexedDB and add storage metrics.
Refine node ports, additive selection, center snapping, toolbar actions,
and workflow dialogs. Stabilize prompt overflow and synchronize pane
height with animated reports to prevent clipped controls.

Cover repository recovery, migration, isolation, editor behavior, and
settings in browser tests; keep pure application logic at full coverage.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:015c06a7764561bb7cc0a817a74863546060a986 -->
### Commit [`015c06a`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/015c06a7764561bb7cc0a817a74863546060a986)

```text
test(ui): stabilize animation and box-selection checks

Observe short animations inside the page before triggering input, so
browser transport delays cannot miss the transition. Retain halfway
content and interpolation assertions before exercising cancellation.

Keep selection rectangles inside the visible gap between operation and
schema nodes after Fit View, preserving exact selected-node assertions.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:6fb7036f6ebfaa02df1957457e651dba661f853d -->
### Commit [`6fb7036`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/6fb7036f6ebfaa02df1957457e651dba661f853d)

```text
fix(workflow): recover interrupted saves before newer drafts

Catalogue finalization can fail after Git has committed an edit. Retain
that exact submitted snapshot and expected revision, recover it first,
and only then save the newer editor draft against the recovered revision.
Keep unresolved attempts dirty and clear them when changing documents.

Preserve the existing stale-tab conflict checks instead of adopting the
latest repository revision blindly. Cover interrupted finalization,
continued editing, one-click retry, and both historical snapshots in the
browser regression.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:bca49a923f80ebf464fc042f24af98c07224a091 -->
### Commit [`bca49a9`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/bca49a923f80ebf464fc042f24af98c07224a091)

```text
feat: expand browser-local workflow editing and settings

Add independent Git-backed schema management, worker-backed persistence,
resource history, locale settings, and themed development diagnostics.

Model HTTP response envelopes and editable additional properties, arrange
new workflow trees using measured node sizes, and refine editor controls,
dialogs, loading transitions, and saved-workflow management.

Update browser and source regressions, centralize Node coverage scope, and
include isolation headers and logging dependencies in release publication.

```

## 0.0.37

<!-- changelog-commit:c3cb00fe85328a17c1f69bcbeb26e28d7924a98c -->
### Commit [`c3cb00f`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/c3cb00fe85328a17c1f69bcbeb26e28d7924a98c)

```text
fix: label the test report CI link with its run ID

Supply an HTML anchor through the reporter's existing customData.ciPipeline
field before report generation. Keep the CI Pipeline heading and full run
URL, displaying only the run ID as the link text. Validate the HTTPS run
URL and escape its attribute value; local reports omit absent CI metadata.

Extend the existing report-generation regression test to check the link.
No report post-processing, dependency, workflow, README or coverage changes.

Validation: both modified files pass Node syntax checks and 11 focused
metadata assertions pass. Full reporter generation and Cucumber tests were
not run locally because the dependencies are unavailable.
```

<!-- changelog-commit:6054c7aa926915852ae1acf46cb277fe81149d02 -->
### Commit [`6054c7a`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/6054c7aa926915852ae1acf46cb277fe81149d02)

```text
fix: simplify test report metadata and capture browser identity

Display seven-character commit hashes and suppress the Username field.
Clear the footer through the reporter's pageFooter and customStyle options.
Keep the existing run-ID CI link and standalone report wrapper unchanged.

Capture Playwright's actual browser name and version in Cucumber hooks on
every browser scenario, including successful runs and parallel workers.
Pass this through native feature metadata before report generation without
rewriting generated HTML or changing original Cucumber results. Consume
metadata-only attachments without displaying them; preserve screenshots
and diagnostics. Label known non-browser suites Not applicable, and reject
invalid or conflicting browser metadata rather than inventing an identity.

Extend the existing report regression scenario for these changes. No new
scenarios, dependencies, workflows, README or coverage-report changes.

Validation: all three modified files pass Node syntax and whitespace
checks. Thirteen focused local tests pass for metadata extraction, hook
capture, scoping, original-input preservation, invalid/conflicting data,
and reporter configuration. These tests use a stubbed reporter and browser;
full reporter rendering, Cucumber and real-browser validation remain
pending because dependencies are unavailable in this environment.
```

<!-- changelog-commit:1ce8b32dad2555f6b4834c26e9c32c30453b4a49 -->
### Commit [`1ce8b32`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/1ce8b32dad2555f6b4834c26e9c32c30453b4a49)

```text
fix: validate live target refs before automatic merges

Read and validate the canonical target ref instead of trusting potentially
stale PR base metadata. Recheck the live ref immediately before either
mutation and fail closed on missing, malformed or unavailable ref data.
Preserve verified dependency-badge advances, existing merge policies,
head identity checks, merge confirmation and deployment tree verification.

Extend existing merge regression checks with independent live-ref data and
22 stale/changing/malformed-ref assertions. Update release-wrapper fixtures
for the two additional canonical reads; scenario counts are unchanged.

Validation: reproduced the unsafe stale-base behavior with the original
script and verified rejection before any write with the patch. Executed
66 existing merge test cases plus 22 live-ref assertions using real shell
helpers and mocked GitHub responses, with a local step-registration shim.
These are focused checks, not a full local Cucumber run. Bash/Node syntax
and whitespace checks pass. Full automation and browser suites await CI.

This is the code-side correction, not an atomic check-and-merge operation.
Native squash/merge still needs server-side protection enforced for the
merging App. Keep the PR draft until that enforcement and CI are reviewed.
No coverage-report, README, workflow or repository-settings changes.
```

<!-- changelog-commit:0abf25d850d9aa8526813d11c90c9be440637791 -->
### Commit [`0abf25d`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/0abf25d850d9aa8526813d11c90c9be440637791)

```text
fix: preserve navigation in standalone coverage reports

Replace coverage-single-html and its minification step with a local,
dependency-free packager that keeps each Istanbul page and its assets in
an isolated document. Route folders, files and named source-line anchors
through the outer document, including reload and browser history.

Add report:coverage for local generation and HTTP/file browser regression
fixtures. Leave README work and the Cucumber report generator unchanged.

Validation: Node syntax, deterministic bundle integrity, UTF-8/filename
preservation, invalid inputs, stale-output cleanup and secret redaction.
Browser navigation was blocked by the local browser policy. Browser and
full-suite validation, including scenario badge regeneration, remain
pending before a PR is opened.
```

<!-- changelog-commit:8e81fb42e4c4dc15dfc9f1c4c07490394f6549d6 -->
### Commit [`8e81fb4`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/8e81fb42e4c4dc15dfc9f1c4c07490394f6549d6)

```text
fix: load standalone coverage pages safely over HTTP and local files

Integrate develop without rewriting branch history, preserving the npm
script rename, alphabetical ordering, report metadata and merge-base fix.

Replace blob page/resource URLs with srcdoc pages and embedded data URLs.
Bind handlers only to the expected loaded document and defer scrolling
until that document is ready. Retain the outer file/line hash navigation.

Keep the existing HTTP/file navigation assertions and exercise real output
from the installed c8 reporter in both scenarios, including sorting,
filtering, source lines, Unicode, reload and browser history. Reject blob
and unexpected resource requests. No extra dependencies or scenarios.

Local verification: JavaScript syntax and in-memory Chromium loading pass.
HTTP and file navigation are blocked by local browser policy; the draft
PR CI must verify both transports and the real c8 reporter before review.
```

### Commit [`11683df`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/11683df503cca2732168a7645575c7bf241f5f41)

```text
feat: add OpenAPI operation workspaces and executable workflows

Replace the initial canvas prototype with prompt-based operation discovery,
schema-driven inputs and reports, and a workflow editor with typed field
connections, reusable saved plans, utility nodes, and explicit execution.

Keep example.com requests local and mocked while offering a separate
httpbin catalog for live request testing. Preserve legacy operation IDs
in saved workflows and validate imported documents before using them.

licensed fonts, and make navigation, forms, tooltips, JSON disclosures,
and canvas controls usable across desktop and mobile layouts.

Gate the built standalone application with browser scenarios alongside
the source, acceptance, and automation suites. Include browser results
in release reports and scenario badges so the deployed bundle receives
the same behavioral checks as the local application.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:9ec3aa8a4a04fce3d1c7ce0f071cc73ae7617c45 -->
### Commit [`9ec3aa8`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/9ec3aa8a4a04fce3d1c7ce0f071cc73ae7617c45)

```text
Revise and condense Code of Conduct

Condensed the Code of Conduct to focus on key commitments, expected conduct, and prohibited behaviors while maintaining essential reporting and enforcement guidelines.

Signed-off-by: Erik Landvall <superhero@superduper.solutions>
```

<!-- changelog-commit:dcd9cb2eaa0312f34936f6efccf54de3470dad86 -->
### Commit [`dcd9cb2`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/dcd9cb2eaa0312f34936f6efccf54de3470dad86)

```text
ci: preview branch builds and reuse unchanged Pages deployments

Publish tested bundles for develop and release/hotfix branches within
existing CI runs. Reuse the active Pages deployment when the final HTML
is unchanged, while preserving exact merge and branch freshness checks.

Use the same release artifact for preview and production. Verify Pages
completion before the GitHub App records success, and record failures
with deployment context. Cover reuse, supersession, retries, and API
failures in the shared Cucumber suite.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:b49bd08dc57b96b389327429c60dd59f0298c8d7 -->
### Commit [`b49bd08`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/b49bd08dc57b96b389327429c60dd59f0298c8d7)

```text
fix(ci): use supported Pages deployment pagination

Limit Pages deployment queries to 25 entries after the live API rejected 100. Keep the response checks aligned and exercise full and empty pages in the existing Cucumber scenarios.

```

## 0.0.33

<!-- changelog-commit:b68dba1e1c72ab04b9f4a7da32cef243c998ae62 -->
### Commit [`b68dba1`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/b68dba1e1c72ab04b9f4a7da32cef243c998ae62)

```text
ci: keep release validation and deployment in one run

Continue each release PR through confirmed merge, content verification,
tagging, deployment, and synchronization. Use artifacts from successful
jobs in the same run and the actual released SHA for all production writes.

Preserve draft validation and retries of already merged releases, including
queued merges and delayed GitHub metadata. Keep the release-creation PR
separate, prevent PR updates from cancelling deployment, and remove the
obsolete cross-run release and artifact discovery helpers.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:6a4a925020f37842cc6b16bcad401022abb18d9d -->
### Commit [`6a4a925`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/6a4a925020f37842cc6b16bcad401022abb18d9d)

```text
fix(ci): confirm merges and accept safe retries

Request merges directly and require GitHub to confirm the exact PR and merge metadata before reporting success. Recheck both mutation paths and recognize already completed merges without another write, including lost-response races.

Preserve native failure reasons with branch, revision, timeout, and write-state context. Pass the expected source through each branch workflow and share the release confirmation deadline. Cover completion, identity changes, retries, and failures in the existing Cucumber suite.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:be8ad2f26ec4df4482aa9aa323ef0139f88fdb15 -->
### Commit [`be8ad2f`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/be8ad2f26ec4df4482aa9aa323ef0139f88fdb15)

```text
refactor(ci): remove duplicate release merge polling

Let the shared merge helper own completion polling. Recheck the exact PR
once before publication, retaining the actual merge SHA and current-main
guards. Cover an unconfirmed final read in the existing Cucumber suite.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:e3f7b3ff9dd9390371dcfa891169f9d903c48f7c -->
### Commit [`e3f7b3f`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/e3f7b3ff9dd9390371dcfa891169f9d903c48f7c)

```text
build: remove unused tailwind-merge dependency

The installed tailwind-variants and cn packages provide the class-merging implementations used by the webpage. Remove the unused external package together with its README entry and version badge.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:a5690fcfcf1c92a4c90a57021e41de3a67ee715b -->
### Commit [`a5690fc`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/a5690fcfcf1c92a4c90a57021e41de3a67ee715b)

```text
ci: preserve Gitflow across badge and hotfix synchronization

Refresh branch-local dependency SVG colours from the scheduled npm check.
Publish only changed badges through the existing App, bind writes to the
checked commit, and skip badge-only main pushes during deployment. Shared
revision guards allow verified badge updates without accepting untested
code or superseded releases.

Synchronize released hotfixes into active releases through the existing
main workflow, restoring their branch at the tagged merge when necessary.
Keep source-content, version-reservation, and support patch-line checks
while allowing released hotfixes to reach their intended targets. Document
the separate protected-branch rules and unchanged version policy.

Keep the coupled workflow, guard, Cucumber, and generated badge changes
together so branch automation and its checked reports remain consistent.

```

```text
chore(deps): update cucumber and lucide
```

<!-- changelog-commit:f38267352eac6c468c74c5efdb12ac8e2caafbf1 -->
### Commit [`f382673`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/f38267352eac6c468c74c5efdb12ac8e2caafbf1)

```text
fix(ci): prefer fast-forward integration and preserve release history

Fast-forward validated single-commit feature and production hotfix PRs; squash their other changes. Preserve ancestry for releases and synchronization, using merge commits when fast-forwarding is unavailable.

Confirm indirect PR merges before resolving release artifacts, retain release preparation and hotfix history across squash merges, and cover retries, stale heads, badge advances, and merge recognition with Cucumber.

```

## 0.0.31

<!-- changelog-commit:54c7d52de08d3e7a0eefe3c73a35dc7fa892cac3 -->
### Commit [`54c7d52`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/54c7d52de08d3e7a0eefe3c73a35dc7fa892cac3)

```text
ci: report dependency checks for all branches in Actions

Discover repository branches dynamically and check their pinned revisions with
the automation from the workflow revision. Report outdated package versions in
job summaries and logs, retaining independent checks and useful error context.

Remove dependency status uploads to R2 and the remote README summary badges.
Keep package-version badges in the repository and preserve release coverage
publishing. Update Cucumber coverage and contributor documentation.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:9cfa325e487b88055bd9bd3428fcb1a4cba57144 -->
### Commit [`9cfa325`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/9cfa325e487b88055bd9bd3428fcb1a4cba57144)

```text
ci: publish bundled test reports with branch badges

Combine source, acceptance, and automation results from the validated release head into one standalone Cucumber HTML report. Require and promote that report alongside coverage through the existing release workflow.

Keep coverage and scenario badges in the repository so their values follow the viewed branch, check them against test results before release, and link them to the published HTML reports. Stop uploading coverage badge JSON to R2.

```

## 0.0.30

<!-- changelog-commit:ce0164d2223271e8acb21482be7324b90a100e8a -->
### Commit [`ce0164d`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/ce0164d2223271e8acb21482be7324b90a100e8a)

```text
ci: remove Actionlint from branch workflows

Keep ShellCheck and Cucumber automation checks in the existing branch workflows. Remove the Docker-based YAML lint step and its input metadata exceptions.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:fd8a3f5730eb50c09d76dd0d8258dcebf8262da0 -->
### Commit [`fd8a3f5`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/fd8a3f5730eb50c09d76dd0d8258dcebf8262da0)

```text
ci: isolate dependency refreshes in a scheduled workflow

Run npm outdated for main and develop daily at 06:00 UTC or by manual
 dispatch through cron-outdated. Keep dependency refreshes off PR and push
 events so branch CI remains focused on validation and releases.

Share badge generation and publishing through a Bash helper with fresh
 temporary files, bounded redacted diagnostics, and Cucumber coverage.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:f3de151fce5973be41a24334ebed066f84c8d83c -->
### Commit [`f3de151`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/f3de151fce5973be41a24334ebed066f84c8d83c)

```text
fix: keep dependency badges with their source revision

Store generated version badges with the code so branch and release READMEs
show their own declared dependencies. Validate the SVGs against the
manifest during CI and cover the README links with Cucumber.

Publish only the explicitly labelled main and develop freshness summaries
from the dependency cron. Name scheduled/manual runs and allow manual
launches from either branch without introducing bot commits.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:aadb34f13d22c8b3e491d6190d2e242fc95f0a4c -->
### Commit [`aadb34f`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/aadb34f13d22c8b3e491d6190d2e242fc95f0a4c)

```text
fix: make badge argument validation explicit

Use an explicit conditional so the hosted runner ShellCheck accepts the
argument validation without suppressing SC2015.

```

## 0.0.29

<!-- changelog-commit:0c226df0c7f1213a5a2e5c176aa5f3e4e760ca77 -->
### Commit [`0c226df`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/0c226df0c7f1213a5a2e5c176aa5f3e4e760ca77)

```text
ci: combine main validation and release deployment

Handle pull request checks and main-push deployment in ci-main.
Keep their jobs and cancellation groups independent, while preserving
validated artifact selection and serialized production writes.

Remove the separate deployment workflow and update guidance and badges.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:b208278950ae9b1bda043ac610a639875208a3a8 -->
### Commit [`b208278`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/b208278950ae9b1bda043ac610a639875208a3a8)

```text
test: migrate automation checks to Cucumber

Use the existing Cucumber runner for release, Gitflow and status checks.
Consolidate repetitive cases into behavior scenarios and replace Python
fixtures with shared JavaScript command doubles that reject unexpected
requests while exercising the real shell scripts.

Emit compatible JSON reports and retain them in CI on success and failure
so automation results can join the application reports later.

```

<!-- changelog-commit:3674853193b93278fd09f4d48eeb664667a55495 -->
### Commit [`3674853`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/3674853193b93278fd09f4d48eeb664667a55495)

```text
ci: give each target branch its own workflow

Separate develop, release and support CI while keeping the combined main
validation and deployment workflow. Each branch file owns its orchestration
and uses the shared Bash scripts for Gitflow and release operations.

Inline workflow checks, retain merge guards and reports, and update the
documentation, badge and narrowly scoped Actionlint workaround.

```

## 0.0.28

<!-- changelog-commit:bdedc486793b1fc2b297fd6d97446c28bc52fb2a -->
### Commit [`bdedc48`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/bdedc486793b1fc2b297fd6d97446c28bc52fb2a)

```text
ci: trigger release deployment only when main changes

Resolve the merged release or hotfix PR from the exact pushed commit
before tagging and promoting its validated head artifacts. Reject missing
or ambiguous release identity with useful input context.

Closing a develop release-trigger PR no longer creates an empty CD run.
Keep app-owned writes, production freshness guards and normal Gitflow.

```

## 0.0.27

<!-- changelog-commit:dc92524dfbf47a2ced31af8a2a2fbcf02197c8b4 -->
### Commit [`dc92524`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/dc92524dfbf47a2ced31af8a2a2fbcf02197c8b4)

```text
ci: simplify release automation and improve failure diagnostics

Consolidate branch validation and application checks, share Gitflow policy,
and promote the exact release artifacts through one production job. Use
the existing GitHub App for authored GitHub writes and share R2 publishing
without routine cleanup requests or duplicate summaries.

Bind automatic merges to the tested head and base, reserve versions across
merge-to-tag retries, and reject superseded production releases. Preserve
native failure reasons with relevant nonsecret context and retain failed
test reports. Document the app permissions and deployment retry behavior.

```

### Commit [`71b7373`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/71b7373efdf462d1cf9473ea46f8e5242c10ac90)

```text
ci: make release workflows retry-safe

Reuse existing releases on retries and wait for completed release CI
before promoting its bundle and coverage artifacts.

Require coverage publishing inputs before merge, preserve producer cache
keys across partial retries, and serialize production promotion with
stale-release guards. Add offline regression tests and workflow linting.

```

## 0.0.15

<!-- changelog-commit:77a998d6fae9dfd1619c837d9cfdafc27b1897b4 -->
### Commit [`77a998d`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/77a998d6fae9dfd1619c837d9cfdafc27b1897b4)

```text
refactor(ci): consolidate main validation and release in main.yml

Keep release artifact promotion and non-gating back-merge unchanged.
Rename Development CI and its PR-direction job for clarity.
```

<!-- changelog-commit:12f0932cf29bc388db7b16b705de320ada66ff5b -->
### Commit [`12f0932`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/12f0932cf29bc388db7b16b705de320ada66ff5b)

```text
fix(ci): validate development pull requests before merging

Run type checks, tests, and build on ordinary develop PRs, not pushes.
Keep the same-repository main back-merge exception and explicit health dispatch.
Use read-only pull_request jobs and retain the required Gitflow check name.
```

<!-- changelog-commit:594dcfacd79ecda69c851889769a220cbc6dbbe2 -->


### Commit [`36d66b6`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/36d66b6ff1413736b693f99ef7b2b8d267b1a526)

```text
chore(ci): label main PR and push runs explicitly
```

<!-- changelog-commit:c193c22bdbba1c074872aa536aaaf425f08a09d6 -->
### Commit [`c193c22`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/c193c22bdbba1c074872aa536aaaf425f08a09d6)

```text
chore(ci): label develop PR and health-check runs explicitly
```

<!-- changelog-commit:9f62d4ff15becdc31b0d24406d5172b2fa37b70e -->
### Commit [`9f62d4f`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/9f62d4ff15becdc31b0d24406d5172b2fa37b70e)

```text
chore(ci): use the requested Main PR workflow name
```

<!-- changelog-commit:8e775efc4f819ae4db95df0ff68ed025b442bffe -->
### Commit [`8e775ef`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/8e775efc4f819ae4db95df0ff68ed025b442bffe)

```text
refactor(ci): use PR-prefixed names and split release/support policy

Name workflows PR-main, PR-develop, PR-release, and PR-support.
Replace 05-gitflow-policy.yml with destination-specific release.yml and support.yml.
Preserve required check contexts, main release behavior, and the non-gating back-merge.
```

<!-- changelog-commit:074887566e9e57a4f6734ab2f18b3d56137d2bfc -->
### Commit [`0748875`](https://github.com/superhero/operations.superduper.solutions/commit/074887566e9e57a4f6734ab2f18b3d56137d2bfc)

```text
refactor(ci): use PR-prefixed workflow files and release on merged PRs

Rename workflows to PR-main.yml, PR-develop.yml, PR-release.yml and PR-support.yml.
Replace the main push trigger with pull_request.closed guarded by merged=true.
Keep pre-merge validation separate from release work and reuse the validated bundle.
Update artifact lookup and development health dispatch to the new filenames.
Retain explicit manual/health dispatches and existing required check names.

Validated four YAML files, 29 shell blocks, 100 event/job predicates,
eight release-context cases and four artifact-selection cases locally.
```
